<script>
		function equalHeights(className) {
			$("."+className).height('auto');
			if($(window).width()>767) {
				var heights = [];
				$("."+className).each(function(index,element) {
					heights.push(element.offsetHeight);
				});
			    var maxHeight = Math.max.apply(null, heights);
			    $("."+className).height(maxHeight);
			}
		}

		function mainHeaderAlignment() {
			var hedSize = $('.hero-banner h1').text().length;
			var divSize = $('.hero-banner').width();
			var ww = $(window).width();
			//console.log('Hed: ' + hedSize);
			if (ww > 991) {
				if (hedSize > 90) {
					$('.hero-banner h1').css('font-size', '28px');
					$('.hero-banner h1').css('line-height', '30px');
				}
				if (hedSize > 65) {
					marginSize = (250 - ((hedSize/2) * 10));
					if (marginSize < 20) {
						marginSize = 20;
					}
					//$('.hero-banner h1').css('padding-right', marginSize);
				} else {
					//$('.hero-banner h1').css('padding-right', 250);
				}
			} else {
				//$('.hero-banner h1').css('padding-right', 20);
			}
		}

    	$(function() {

		$(document).ready(function(){
			$(".signupsubmit").on("click",function(e){
				e.preventDefault;
				$(".signupanswer").html("Thank you for signing up for Inc. Must Reads!");
				var email = $('.signupinput').val();
				if (email == '') {
					$(".signupanswer").html("Please enter your email address");
				} else {
					if( Inc.Utilities.isValidEmailAddress(email) ) {
						$.ajax({
						  type: "POST",
						  data:{"formType": "HomepageNews", "email": email, "country":pageInfo.geo.countryname},
						  url:"/rest/newhomenewsletters",
						  success:function(params) {
							if(params.overallstatus == "OK"){
								$(".signupanswer").html("Thank you for signing up for Inc. Must Reads!");
								$(".signupinteract").css("display", "none");
								$(".signupanswer").css("padding-right", "18%");
							}
							else{$(".signupanswer").html("Something went wrong...");}
						  }
					   });
					}else{$(".signupanswer").html("Please enter a valid email address");}
				}
			});
		});

	inc.currentController.nativeTile= function(targeting) {
		//make a rest call either campaign name, or article ids

		//console.log( "Targeting:", targeting);
		//console.log( "ExcludeIDs:", inc.currentController.excludeIds);

		if(targeting.campaign != "default") {

			$.ajax({
		       type: "GET",
		       url: "/rest/nativetile",
		       data: { campaign: targeting.campaign, sort: targeting.sort, articleIds: targeting.article_ids, nativetype: targeting.nativetype, clickthrough: targeting.clickthrough_url, excludeIds: inc.currentController.excludeIds }
		     }).done(function(data) {

		     	data = data.data;

		     	$.each(data, function(key,value) {
		     		if(window.inc.currentController.excludeIds.indexOf(value.id)==-1) {
		     			data = value;

				     		}

								}
								txt += data.brandview+'<span class="view">view</span>';
								if(data.brandviewhub) {
									txt += '</a>';
								}
								txt += '</div>';
				     		}

	try {
		adController.on('slotRenderEnded', function(event) {
			var slotId = event.slot.getSlotElementId();
			// console.log("======= "+slotId+" =======");
			// console.log(event);

			// Custom Welcome Mat template Hack for Nate added: 6/29/18
			if(event.slot.getHtml().includes('welcome mat video')) {
				$('.main-header').removeClass('hold-in-place');
				$('.main-header').addClass('spotible-showing');
				inc.controllers[0].topNavigation.stayInView = false;
				inc.controllers[0].topNavigation.handlePosition();
			}

			// IF this is the OOP slot and the ad request size is a 1x1...
			if ( slotId === 'oop' && !_.isNull(event.size) && (event.size[0] == 1 && event.size[1] == 1) ) {
				// Skip standard timeout and un-fix top nav
				$('.main-header').removeClass('hold-in-place');
				$('.main-header').addClass('spotible-showing');
				inc.controllers[0].topNavigation.stayInView = false;
				inc.controllers[0].topNavigation.handlePosition();

				// Hide standard leaderboard if OOP slot displayed
				$('.leaderboard-container').css('visibility','hidden');

				// Hide certain elements for full display
				//$('#TopNav #leaderboard-container').html('');
				$('#TopNav #leaderboard-container').css({
					'height': '0px',
					'padding': '0px'
				});
				$('#adhesive_banner').hide();
			}
			
	    	if ( slotId === 'flexiblepushdown') {
				// IF this is the 600x600 flex pushdown, then immediately remove Fixerd top nav
				if( !_.isNull(event.size) && (event.size[0] == 600 && event.size[1] == 600) ){
					$('.main-header').removeClass('hold-in-place');
					inc.controllers[0].topNavigation.stayInView = false;
					inc.controllers[0].topNavigation.handlePosition();
				}
				if($('.main-header').hasClass('hold-in-place')) {
					inc.controllers[0].topNavigation.handlePosition();
					setTimeout(_.bind(function(){
						$('.main-header').removeClass('hold-in-place');
						inc.controllers[0].topNavigation.stayInView = false;
						inc.controllers[0].topNavigation.handlePosition();
					}, this), 6000);
				}
	    	}

	    	if ( slotId === 'fixedlogo' && event.isEmpty ) {
	        	$("section.main-features .side-heroes div:nth-child(5)").show();
	    	}

	    	if( slotId === 'native_feature' && event.isEmpty ) {
				$('.nativesubfeature').show();
			}
		});
    	adController.start();
	}
	catch(e) {
    	console.log(e);
	}

	        }

	}
	catch(e) {
    	console.log(e);
	}
